        "lines": [
          "     Symbolic Consensus in LLM Reasoning",
          "   core_claim: >",
          "     Same-model self-extraction of symbolic constraints inherits trace",
          "-    correlation (Fleiss' κ=0.21–0.82, Eff-K=1.20–3.62 for nominal K=12),",
          "-    creating a structural independence bottleneck. Oracle gold-FOL yields",
          "-    +14.22pp (p<0.001) proving aggregation is sound, but self-extraction",
          "-    captures only 24%. κ generalization across aggregation methods reveals",
          "-    a fundamental independence-accuracy trade-off: Debate reduces κ from",
          "-    0.44→0.15 (Eff-K 2→4.4) but collapses accuracy by -18.6pp. Same-model",
          "-    methods cannot increase independence without degrading the signal that",
          "-    makes aggregation useful.",
          "-  paper_type: \"Fundamental limitation analysis (Condorcet framework + empirical diagnosis + 12 remediation failures)\"",
          "-  framing: \"Independence Bottleneck — structural limitation of same-model self-extraction\"",
          "+    correlation, creating a structural independence bottleneck rooted in",
          "+    Symbolic Confirmation Bias: self-extracted FOL constraints systematically",
          "+    confirm trace answers (bias ratio 4.97× on incorrect problems), making",
          "+    symbolic aggregation equivalent to majority voting. Process-level analysis",
          "+    reveals reasoning paths are far more homogeneous than answers suggest",
          "+    (cosine similarity 0.69 vs answer-κ 0.41). The independence-accuracy",
          "+    trade-off is fundamental: Debate reduces κ from 0.44→0.15 but collapses",
          "+    accuracy by -18.6pp. 25+ interventions spanning all dimensions fail.",
          "+    Oracle gold-FOL proves aggregation works (+14.22pp, p<0.001); self-",
          "+    extraction captures only 24%.",
          "+  paper_type: >",
          "+    Comprehensive negative result + diagnostic analysis (Condorcet framework",
          "+    + Symbolic Confirmation Bias root cause + 25+ exhaustive remediation",
          "+    failures + novel process-κ metric)",
          "+  framing: >",
          "+    D085 FINAL: Independence bottleneck is structural, universal across",
          "+    architectures, and rooted in Symbolic Confirmation Bias. 25+ interventions",
          "+    spanning all conceivable dimensions fail. Process-κ reveals reasoning",
          "+    homogeneity far exceeds answer-level agreement.",
          "   created_at: 2026-05-19T20:30:00Z",
          "+  updated_at: 2026-05-20T10:00:00Z",
          "+  previous_version: \"v1 (2026-05-19): 5 contributions, 12 remediations\"",
          "+  change_summary: >",
          "+    v1→v2: Added C7 (Symbolic Confirmation Bias) and C8 (Process-κ) from",
          "+    D082. Expanded remediation from 12 to 25+ (D080/D085 wave 1-3).",
          "+    Restructured contributions from 5→4 (consolidated). Analysis section",
          "+    restructured to feature confirmation bias and process-κ prominently.",
          "+    Universal across 3 model families (D078).",
          " ",
          " # ──────────────────────────────────────────────",
          "-# Contributions (5)",
          "+# Contributions (4, consolidating 8 internal claims)",
          " # ──────────────────────────────────────────────",
          " contributions:",
          "   - id: C1",
          "-    label: \"Independence Bottleneck Quantification\"",
          "+    label: \"Independence-Accuracy Trade-off Is Fundamental (PRIMARY)\"",
          "     status: VERIFIED",
          "+    internal_claims: [C4_old, C6_new]",
          "     claim: >",
          "-      Fleiss' κ=0.21–0.82 (instruction-tuned), 0.91 (thinking mode).",
          "-      Eff-K=1.20–3.62 at K=12. Mistral 4-seed κ=0.41–0.51 (stable).",
          "-      Qwen Eff-K≈1.2–1.5 → near-zero Δ; Mistral PW Eff-K=3.62 but",
          "-      SC=39% → negative Δ. Dual requirement: sufficient Eff-K AND SC.",
          "-    evidence: \"Table 3, §5.1\"",
          "+      κ generalization across 4 aggregation methods reveals a fundamental",
          "+      Pareto frontier: SC (κ=0.44), Diverse-Prompt (κ=0.40), and ICG",
          "+      (κ=0.47) cluster at κ≈0.40–0.47 (same-model ceiling); Debate",
          "+      reduces κ to 0.15 (Eff-K=4.44) but collapses accuracy by -18.6pp",
          "+      on FOLIO and -23.0pp on LogiQA. Three model families (Mistral,",
          "+      LLaMA, Qwen) all produce κ≥0.41, confirming the ceiling is",
          "+      architecture-independent. 25+ interventions spanning 8 dimensions",
          "+      (diversity/architecture/capability/verification/filtering/voting/",
          "+      solver/paradigm) — including 9 prompt-level, 3 method-level, and",
          "+      13 additional directions — all fail. This constrains all same-model",
          "+      trace aggregation, not only symbolic approaches.",
          "+    evidence: \"Table 5 (κ generalization), Table 4 (method interventions), §5.4, Appendix remediation tables\"",
          "+    registry_refs:",
          "+      - exp-077  # κ generalization",
          "+      - exp-076  # Debate FOLIO",
          "+      - exp-078  # Debate LogiQA",
          "+      - exp-074  # ICG FOLIO",
          "+      - exp-075b # Contrastive FOLIO",
          "+      - exp-cross-model-phase1  # Cross-model κ",
          "+      - exp-cross-model-phase2  # Cross-model SICA",
          "+      # Plus 13 wave 1-3 experiments (see remediation section)",
          " ",
          "   - id: C2",
          "-    label: \"Oracle Gap Localizes Extraction Bottleneck\"",
          "+    label: \"Symbolic Confirmation Bias as Root Cause (CORE)\"",
          "     status: VERIFIED",
          "+    internal_claims: [C7_new]",
          "     claim: >",
          "-      Gold FOL: +14.22pp FOLIO (p<0.001), +11.50pp PW (p<0.001).",
          "-      Self-extraction captures 24% of oracle ceiling (best seed +3.43pp",
          "-      vs +14.22pp). Aggregation mechanism sound; extraction quality is",
          "-      the binding bottleneck.",
          "-    evidence: \"Table 1 oracle rows, §4.3\"",
          "+      Self-extracted FOL constraints systematically confirm the trace's",
          "+      own (possibly incorrect) answer rather than independently verifying",
          "+      ground truth. On SICA-incorrect problems, 83.3% of constraints",
          "+      support the wrong answer (bias ratio 4.97×). 97.6% of SICA errors",
          "+      overlap with SC errors. The 12 SICA-correct flips come from minority",
          "+      trace diversity (43% gold support), not symbolic reasoning. Oracle",
          "+      gold-FOL proves aggregation is sound (+14.22pp FOLIO, +11.50pp PW,",
          "+      p<0.001); self-extraction captures only 24% of this ceiling. The",
          "+      causal chain: model reasons → answer → FOL extraction confirms",
          "+      answer → aggregation ≈ SC.",
          "+    evidence: \"§5.2, §4.3 (Oracle), Table 1 oracle rows\"",
          "+    registry_refs:",
          "+      - exp-confirmation-bias",
          "+      - exp-031  # Oracle FOLIO",
          "+      - exp-022  # Oracle PW",
          "+      - exp-direction-d-cegis  # CEGIS validates: consistency ≠ bottleneck",
          " ",
          "   - id: C3",
          "-    label: \"Regime-Dependent Effect with 4-Seed Replication\"",
          "+    label: \"Process-κ Reveals Deeper Independence Bottleneck (NEW METRIC)\"",
          "     status: VERIFIED",
          "+    internal_claims: [C8_new, C1_old]",
          "     claim: >",
          "-      Intermediate-SC (~50–55%): best seed +3.43pp (p=0.092, ns);",
          "-      4-seed mean +1.47pp; 2/4 marginal (p<0.10), 0/4 significant.",
          "-      Type A (SC≥60%): 9 conditions N>2,800, no significant positive;",
          "-      significant negatives at -2.50pp (p=0.011) and -4.41pp (p=0.093).",
          "-      Type C (SC≤40%): -2.83pp (p=0.014).",
          "-    evidence: \"Table 1, Figure 2, §4.1–4.3\"",
          "+      Answer-level κ underestimates the true independence bottleneck.",
          "+      CoT reasoning similarity (cosine=0.69) far exceeds answer-κ (0.41),",
          "+      with near-zero correlation (r=-0.06, p=0.36). Only 9.3% of trace",
          "+      pairs exhibit convergent reasoning (same answer, different process).",
          "+      This metric generalizes beyond SICA to all LLM trace aggregation",
          "+      methods. Combined with answer-κ quantification: Fleiss' κ=0.21–0.82",
          "+      (Eff-K=1.20–3.62 at K=12) across 15 conditions; three model families",
          "+      all produce κ≥0.41, confirming universality.",
          "+    evidence: \"§3.4 (definition), §5.3 (results), Table 3\"",
          "+    registry_refs:",
          "+      - exp-wild4-process-kappa",
          "+      - exp-cross-model-phase1  # Cross-model κ",
          " ",
          "   - id: C4",
          "-    label: \"Independence-Accuracy Trade-off Is Fundamental\"",
          "+    label: \"Regime-Dependent Pattern and Zero-Cost Diagnostics\"",
          "     status: VERIFIED",
          "+    internal_claims: [C3_old, C5_old]",
          "     claim: >",
          "-      κ generalization (exp-077) reveals why same-model methods must fail:",
          "-      SC κ=0.44 (Eff-K=2.02); Diverse-Prompt κ=0.40 (Eff-K=2.21, ≈SC);",
          "-      Debate κ=0.15 (Eff-K=4.44) but Δ=-18.6pp. Methods that increase",
          "-      independence (Debate) destroy accuracy; methods that preserve accuracy",
          "-      (ICG, Diverse-Prompt) cannot break correlation. 12 remediations",
          "-      (9 prompt + 3 method) all fail — not engineering failure but",
          "-      fundamental trade-off in same-model aggregation.",
          "-    evidence: \"§5.6, Table T7 (κ generalization), method intervention table\"",
          "+      SICA effectiveness depends sharply on SC accuracy regime. A",
          "+      directional positive trend (best +3.43pp, mean +1.47pp; 0/4 seeds",
          "+      significant) appears only in a narrow band (~50–55% SC) on one model.",
          "+      Three failure modes: confirmation bias (Type A, SC≥60%), non-",
          "+      formalizability (Type B), error amplification (Type C, SC≤40%).",
          "+      C0 combined with SC accuracy provides a zero-cost deployment",
          "+      diagnostic. Entropy decomposition confirms: all SICA effects",
          "+      concentrate in high-entropy problems; direction depends on regime.",
          "+    evidence: \"Table 1, Figure 2, §4.1–4.4, Appendix entropy\"",
          "+    registry_refs:",
          "+      - exp-033  # Mistral FOLIO s42 (hero)",
          "+      - exp-052  # s123",
          "+      - exp-053  # s456",
          "+      - exp-067  # s789",
          "+      - exp-046  # Type C",
          " ",
          "-  - id: C5",
          "-    label: \"Entropy Decomposition Reveals Regime Mechanism\"",
          "-    status: VERIFIED",
          "-    claim: >",
          "-      Low-entropy: SICA≈SC across all 8 conditions (Δ≈0). High-entropy",
          "-      direction regime-dependent: +12.8pp intermediate-SC, -8.2pp Type A.",
          "-      Explains dual-channel mechanism (voting diversity + constraint",
          "-      homogenization).",
          "-    evidence: \"§5.5, entropy analysis\"",
          "-",

          "-      - \"Regime: +3.43pp intermediate-SC (ns), -2.83pp Type C (p=0.014), ~0 Type A\"",

          "+      - \"ROOT CAUSE: Symbolic Confirmation Bias — 4.97× bias ratio on incorrect problems, 97.6% SICA errors overlap SC\"",

          "     core_bullets:",
          "-      - \"Hook: Test-time compute scaling via SC improves reasoning—but plateaus. Adding more traces yields diminishing returns.\"",
          "-      - \"Gap: Neuro-symbolic methods (Logic-LM, DyLoS, FoVer) improve reasoning with external/trained components. Can same-model self-extraction of formal constraints break the SC plateau without external tools?\"",
          "-      - \"Insight: CJT requires voter independence. Same-model traces violate this (Eff-K=1.20–3.62 for K=12). Self-extracted constraints inherit the correlation structure.\"",
          "-      - \"Evaluation: SICA pipeline + Condorcet analysis + oracle control across 6 models × 3 domains, 4-seed replication\"",
          "-      - \"Key result: Oracle +14.22pp proves mechanism works; self-extraction captures 24%. 12 remediations all fail.\"",
          "-      - \"Contributions: C1–C5\"",
          "+      - \"¶1 Hook: Test-time compute + SC plateaus (Loo 2025). Same traces = correlated voters.\"",
          "+      - \"¶2 Gap: Neuro-symbolic methods use external components. Can same-model self-extraction break SC plateau?\"",
          "+      - \"¶3 Insight: CJT independence violated. Self-extracted constraints inherit bias — not just correlation but CONFIRMATION BIAS (4.97× ratio). Process-level similarity (cosine 0.69) exceeds answer agreement (κ=0.41).\"",
          "+      - \"¶4 Evaluation: SICA + Condorcet + Oracle + 25+ interventions across 6 models, 3 domains\"",
          "+      - \"¶5 Key results: No significant positive; oracle proves mechanism; independence-accuracy trade-off; 3 model families universal\"",
          "+      - \"Contributions: C1 (trade-off + 25+ methods), C2 (confirmation bias + oracle gap), C3 (process-κ + independence quantification), C4 (regime pattern + diagnostics)\"",

          "     subsections:",
          "       - id: \"sec:condorcet\"",
          "         title: \"Self-Consistency and the Condorcet Independence Assumption\"",
          "         word_budget: 300",
          "+        rewrite: KEEP",
          "         bullets:",
          "-          - \"SC as majority voting: sample K reasoning traces, select most common answer\"",
          "-          - \"CJT: K independent voters each with p>0.5 → P(majority correct) → 1 as K→∞\"",
          "-          - \"LLM traces violate independence: same model, same prompt, only sampling noise\"",
          "-          - \"Eff-K = K/(1+(K-1)κ) via Kish (1965) design effect; κ = Fleiss' kappa (1971)\"",
          "-          - \"When Eff-K≈1, additional traces ≈ no new information → SC plateau\"",
          "+          - \"SC as majority voting; CJT independence guarantee\"",
          "+          - \"LLM traces violate independence\"",
          "+          - \"Eff-K = K/(1+(K-1)κ); Kish (1965) design effect\"",
          "+",
          "       - id: \"sec:sica-pipeline\"",
          "         title: \"SICA Pipeline\"",
          "         word_budget: 400",
          "+        rewrite: KEEP",
          "         bullets:",
          "-          - \"Step 1: Generate K reasoning traces at temperature T\"",
          "-          - \"Step 2: Extract FOL constraints from each trace at T_ext=0.3\"",
          "-          - \"Step 3: Syntactic deduplication across traces\"",
          "-          - \"Step 4: Weighted partial MAX-SAT — hard clauses (answer assertion), soft clauses (constraints weighted by occurrence count)\"",
          "-          - \"Step 5: Z3 solver evaluates each candidate; answer with highest satisfiable weight selected\"",
          "-          - \"Timeout: 10s per candidate answer\"",
          "+          - \"Trace generation → constraint extraction → dedup → MAX-SAT → scoring\"",
          "+          - \"Degeneration under confirmation bias (existing Eq. 4)\"",

          "-      - id: \"sec:method-interventions\"",
          "-        title: \"Method-Level Interventions\"",
          "+          - \"Gold FOL replaces self-extracted; same Z3 pipeline\"",

          "+        title: \"Process-Level Independence\"",

          "+        rewrite: NEW",

          "-          - \"Contrastive: Pro/con constraint scoring per candidate answer\"",

          "-          - \"Each targets a different bottleneck aspect\"",

          "+          - \"Motivation: answer-κ may underestimate true independence bottleneck\"",

          "+        rewrite: KEEP",
          "         bullets:",

          "-          - \"Qwen family: κ=0.66–0.82, Eff-K=1.2–1.5 → all near-zero Δ\"",
          "-          - \"Mistral PW: κ=0.21, Eff-K=3.62 but SC=39% → negative (highest independence, insufficient quality)\"",
          "-          - \"Qwen3-14B thinking: κ=0.91, Eff-K=1.09 → 'better reasoning' worsens independence\"",
          "-          - \"Dual requirement: need both sufficient Eff-K AND sufficient SC\"",
          "-          - \"Explains SC plateau: traces beyond Eff-K provide no new information\"",
          "-      - id: \"sec:extraction-taxonomy\"",
          "-        title: \"Constraint Quality Analysis\"",

          "+        word_budget: 250",

          "         bullets:",
          "-          - \"Discriminative rate: ~8.2% (95% CI [7.6, 8.8])\"",
          "-          - \"Removing non-discriminative constraints: 0 gain → problem is direction not quality\"",
          "-          - \"Variable naming: 56.4% cross-trace constraint pairs share zero Z3 variables\"",
          "-          - \"~92% constraints ARE discriminative but discriminate in same direction as model bias\"",
          "-      - id: \"sec:entropy\"",
          "-        title: \"Entropy Decomposition\"",
          "+          - \"On SICA-correct problems: gold constraint rate 78.1%, bias ratio 0.28\"",
          "+          - \"On SICA-incorrect problems: wrong constraint rate 83.3%, bias ratio 4.97×\"",
          "+          - \"97.6% of SICA errors overlap with SC errors (exp-confirmation-bias: 94%)\"",
          "+          - \"12 SICA-correct flips: minority trace diversity (43% gold support), not symbolic reasoning\"",
          "+          - \"Causal chain: model→answer→FOL confirms answer→aggregation≈SC\"",
          "+          - \"CEGIS validation: iterative refinement R1 +3.43pp < original SICA +4.9pp → consistency ≠ bottleneck, bias is\"",
          "+          - \"Quality Ladder: L1 (cross-model extraction) ≈ L0 (self) << L3 (oracle) → bottleneck in traces not extractor\"",
          "+        registry_refs:",
          "+          - exp-confirmation-bias",
          "+          - exp-direction-d-cegis",

          "+      - id: \"sec:process-kappa\"",
          "+        title: \"Process-Level Independence\"",
          "         word_budget: 200",
          "+        rewrite: NEW",
          "         bullets:",

          "-          - \"Low-entropy: SICA≈SC across all 8 conditions (Δ≈0) — traces agree, constraints add nothing\"",
          "-          - \"High-entropy: regime-dependent — +12.8pp intermediate-SC (s42), -8.2pp Type A (Qwen14B)\"",
          "-          - \"High-entropy proportion: Mistral 38% vs Qwen 18% → explains model-level Δ differences\"",
          "-          - \"Mechanism: low-entropy = direct CJT channel; high-entropy = indirect constraint channel\"",
          "-      - id: \"sec:ablation\"",
          "-        title: \"MAX-SAT Ablation\"",
          "-        word_budget: 100",
          "+          - \"Mistral-7B FOLIO-204: CoT cosine similarity 0.69 >> answer-κ 0.41\"",
          "+          - \"Near-zero correlation: r=-0.06, p=0.36\"",
          "+          - \"Only 9.3% trace pairs show convergent reasoning (same answer, different process)\"",
          "+          - \"Answer-κ is a lower bound on the true independence bottleneck\"",
          "+          - \"Implication: even methods targeting answer diversity may not break process-level correlation\"",
          "+        registry_refs:",

          "-          - \"T=0.7 is isolated spike; only 1/4 temperatures positive\"",

          "-          - \"Figure 3: two-panel K/T sensitivity plot\"",

          "         title: \"Remediation Analysis\"",
          "-        word_budget: 350",

          "+        rewrite: MAJOR_REWRITE",
          "         bullets:",
          "-          - \"--- Prompt-level (9 strategies, 6 dimensions) ---\"",
          "-          - \"Prompting: contrastive extraction (disc_rate 8.2%→6.0%, Δ still mirrors bias)\"",
          "-          - \"Architecture: cross-model bilateral (weak→strong: 0% disc; strong→weak: ±1pp of self)\"",
          "-          - \"Capability: generational scaling Qwen3-14B (+0.49pp ns)\"",
          "-          - \"Verification: Z3 feedback (0.9% UNSAT), cross-trace UNSAT (56.4% zero shared vars)\"",
          "-          - \"Diversity: multi-temp (Δ=0.00pp), T=1.0 (Δ=0.00pp — collapse from +3.43)\"",
          "-          - \"Filtering: discriminative constraint filtering (0 gain), consistency filtering (reverts to SC)\"",
          "-          - \"ICL with gold FOL examples (+0.50pp ns)\"",
          "-          - \"--- Method-level (3 methods × 2 datasets) ---\"",
          "-          - \"ICG (Independent Constraint Gen.): FOLIO Δ=-2.0/+1.5pp (noise), LogiQA -0.5pp → neutral\"",
          "-          - \"  Insight: independent constraints ≈ trace-sourced → bias from problem understanding stage\"",
          "-          - \"Contrastive (Pro/Con Scoring): FOLIO -9.3pp, LogiQA pending → harmful\"",
          "-          - \"  Insight: 26% degenerate scoring, pro/con imbalance (94 pro vs 65 con)\"",
          "-          - \"Debate (Proposer/Critic): FOLIO -18.6pp, LogiQA pending → catastrophic\"",
          "-          - \"  Insight: 7B role-play degradation; same-model debate = same bias + cognitive overhead\"",
          "-          - \"Scale caveat: all method-level tested at 7B; 14B+ may differ\"",
          "-          - \"--- κ Generalization: Independence-Accuracy Trade-off (exp-077, CAPSTONE) ---\"",
          "-          - \"Table T7: SC κ=0.44 Eff-K=2.02 | Diverse-Prompt κ=0.40 Eff-K=2.21 | Debate κ=0.15 Eff-K=4.44\"",
          "-          - \"Diverse-Prompt ≈ SC κ → changing prompts cannot break correlation\"",
          "-          - \"Debate breaks κ (0.44→0.15) but accuracy collapses (-18.6pp)\"",
          "-          - \"FUNDAMENTAL TRADE-OFF: methods that increase independence destroy accuracy; methods that preserve accuracy cannot break correlation\"",
          "-          - \"This explains WHY same-model methods must fail — not just that they do\"",
          "-          - \"Implication: breaking bottleneck requires cross-model or external signal (consistent with oracle)\"",
          "+          - \"--- Prompt-level (9 strategies, 6 dimensions — existing, compressed) ---\"",
          "+          - \"All 9 fail; contrastive best at reducing non-disc (8.2%→6.0%) but Δ still negative\"",
          "+          - \"--- Method-level (3 methods, Table 4 — existing, keep) ---\"",
          "+          - \"ICG: neutral (Δ≈0, 3 seeds); Contrastive: harmful (-9.31pp FOLIO); Debate: catastrophic (-18.63pp)\"",
          "+          - \"--- 13 Additional Directions (NEW, compressed) ---\"",
          "+          - \"PROVE (code verification): 15.2% code gen, accuracy=random\"",
          "+          - \"CEGIS (iterative refinement): R1 +3.43pp < SICA +4.9pp, oscillates\"",
          "+          - \"DeepConf (logprob filtering): zero correlation with correctness\"",
          "+          - \"Cross-model SICA: Δ=-0.49pp despite lower κ\"",
          "+          - \"Problem perturbation: κ increases to 0.696 (anti-effect)\"",
          "+          - \"MIN-UNSAT: 49.5% < SC 54.4%\"",
          "+          - \"κ-aware SC: 11/11 conditions Δ=0\"",
          "+          - \"Z3 trace filter: negative\"",
          "+          - \"Bootstrapped gold: oscillating, no convergence\"",
          "+          - \"Selective abstention: AUROC=0.673, +4.4pp@80% coverage (diagnostic value only)\"",
          "+          - \"ASCII perturbation: κ unchanged (negative)\"",
          "+          - \"Cross-model ensemble (3 families): Δ=-0.49pp\"",

          "   - section: discussion",
          "     file: discussion.tex",
          "-    word_budget: 450",
          "+    word_budget: 400",
          "+    rewrite_scope: MODERATE_REWRITE",
          "+    change_rationale: >",
          "+      Add Symbolic Confirmation Bias as unifying root-cause explanation.",
          "+      Add process-κ implication. Update \"12 remediations\" → \"25+\".",
          "+      Compress existing content to fit within reduced budget.",
          "     core_bullets:",
          "-      - \"Oracle causally isolates: aggregation mechanism sound, extraction is binding bottleneck\"",
          "-      - \"Dual-channel mechanism: direct (voting diversity → SC plateau) + indirect (constraint homogenization → MAX-SAT degeneration)\"",
          "-      - \"Oracle disambiguates: gold constraints on same correlated traces yield +14.22pp → indirect channel is proximal bottleneck\"",
          "-      - \"Independence-accuracy trade-off (exp-077): Debate breaks κ (0.44→0.15) but collapses accuracy (-18.6pp). Diverse-Prompt ≈ SC κ. Same-model methods face a fundamental Pareto frontier.\"",
          "-      - \"This trade-off explains WHY same-model methods must fail, not just that they do. Breaking correlation within a single model's distribution necessarily degrades the signal.\"",
          "-      - \"Published methods (DyLoS, Aristotle, FoVer, Logic-LM) bypass same-model extraction — consistent with trade-off: cross-model/external sources are naturally independent without sacrificing quality\"",
          "-      - \"Statistical evidence: 0/4 seeds significant positive; only significant effects are negative\"",
          "-      - \"C0+SC deployment diagnostic: C0<5%→degenerate, high SC→confirmation bias, low SC→error amplification\"",
          "-      - \"Three paths forward trade simplicity for independence: trained extractors, external provers, multi-model pipelines — all introduce signal outside the model's own distribution\"",
          "+      - \"Oracle causally identifies: aggregation sound, extraction binding\"",
          "+      - \"Symbolic Confirmation Bias as root cause: dual-channel (voting diversity + constraint homogenization) unified under confirmation bias\"",
          "+      - \"Process-κ reveals bottleneck deeper than answer-level: traces similar in reasoning, not just answers\"",
          "+      - \"Independence-accuracy trade-off: Debate breaks κ but destroys accuracy; 25+ interventions confirm\"",
          "+      - \"Statistical evidence: 0 significant positives vs multiple significant negatives\"",
          "+      - \"Published methods (DyLoS, Aristotle, FoVer) bypass self-extraction — consistent with confirmation bias diagnosis\"",
          "+      - \"Scale limitations: κ increases with scale (Qwen 7B→14B); 70B+ open\"",
          "+      - \"Three paths forward: trained extractors, external provers, multi-model\"",
          "     dependencies: [experiments, analysis]",
          " ",
          "   - section: conclusion",
          "     file: conclusion.tex",
          "-    word_budget: 300",
          "+    word_budget: 250",
          "+    rewrite_scope: MODERATE_REWRITE",

          "     core_bullets:",
          "-      - \"SICA evaluated: 6 models spanning 3 families, 3 reasoning domains, N>3,000, 4-seed replication\"",
          "-      - \"Independence bottleneck: κ=0.21–0.82, Eff-K=1.20–3.62 → explains SC plateau and SICA failure\"",
          "-      - \"Intermediate-SC region: +3.43pp best seed (ns), 4-seed mean +1.47pp (2/4 positive, 2/4 null/negative)\"",
          "-      - \"Oracle +14.22pp/+11.50pp (p<0.001); 12 remediations all fail; independence-accuracy trade-off explains why\"",
          "-      - \"Limitations: trained extractors untested, narrow intermediate-SC zone, 7B-scale method testing, formal reasoning domains only\"",
          "+      - \"SICA evaluated: 6 models, 3 families, 3 domains, N>3,000, 4-seed replication\"",
          "+      - \"Root cause: Symbolic Confirmation Bias (4.97× ratio); process-κ (cosine 0.69 >> answer-κ 0.41)\"",
          "+      - \"Independence-accuracy trade-off: Pareto frontier confirmed; 25+ interventions all fail\"",
          "+      - \"Oracle proves mechanism sound; bottleneck structural and universal across 3 architectures\"",
          "+      - \"Diagnostics: C0+SC predictor + Eff-K scaling ceiling\"",
          "+      - \"Limitations: 7B–14B scale, formal reasoning domains; trained extractors and 70B+ open\"",
          "     dependencies: [experiments, analysis]",

  "content": "[Director 指令] [Framing Evaluation] 评估新论文叙事 \"Selective Symbolic Verification\" 是否成立。\n\n**目标会议**: EMNLP 2026 ARR May 25\n\n**当前 framing** (paper_framing.md): \"Regime-Dependent Mechanism Analysis with Entropy Decomposition\" — 纯 analysis paper，5 个 claims，核心是 independence bottleneck + symbolic confirmation bias + 12+ 失败干预 = structural limitation。\n\n**用户提议的新 framing**: \"Selective Symbolic Verification: When (and Why Not) to Trust Symbolic Constraints in LLM Reasoning\"\n\n三层结构：\n1. **诊断层**: 量化两个根本瓶颈 — independence bottleneck (κ=0.21-0.82, Eff-K=1.2-3.6) + symbolic confirmation bias (Bias Ratio=4.97×, 错误题上 constraints 系统性确认错误答案)\n2. **因果链层**: 解释为什么 12+ 种干预全部失败 — 不是方法不对，而是因果链: same model → correlated traces → biased FOL extraction → confirmation bias amplification。打断任何一环的尝试（换模型提取、contrastive prompting、debate、温度调节等）都未能成功\n3. **选择性解决层**: 不是\"symbolic verification 无效\"，而是\"在特定 regime 有效\"。Goldilocks zone (SC~50-56%, κ~0.45-0.50) 有 directional improvement (+2-3pp)；Selective Abstention (AUROC=0.673, +4.4pp@80% coverage) 提供实用诊断价值\n\n**正在进行的新实验**（可能影响 framing）：\n- Direction R (DeBERTa External Verifier): 用 encoder-only NLI 模型作为架构独立验证器，如果成功将为第三层提供更强的 positive result\n- Direction F (Blind FOL): 从 premises-only 提取 FOL，打断 confirmation bias 因果链\n- Direction H (Adversarial Negation): 强制生成反面 constraints\n\n**请评估**：\n1. 新叙事 vs 当前叙事：哪个更适合 EMNLP 2026？\n2. 三层结构是否连贯？是否足以支撑 A 会 (Overall Assessment ≥ 4.0)？\n3. 如果 R (DeBERTa) 成功，framing 该如何调整？如果 R 也失败呢？\n4. 目前 12+ 失败干预 + 3 个 directional positive + entropy decomposition regime analysis 能否撑起 8 页 main conference paper？\n5. 哪些实验结果是当前叙事的 gap？\n\n不需要写论文，只需要评估 framing 可行性并提出建议。引用不需要验证。"

_source line 8911 · 2026-05-20T09:45:47.812Z_

[Director 指令] [Framing Evaluation] 评估新论文叙事 "Selective Symbolic Verification" 是否成立。

**目标会议**: EMNLP 2026 ARR May 25

**当前 framing** (paper_framing.md): "Regime-Dependent Mechanism Analysis with Entropy Decomposition" — 纯 analysis paper，5 个 claims，核心是 independence bottleneck + symbolic confirmation bias + 12+ 失败干预 = structural limitation。

**用户提议的新 framing**: "Selective Symbolic Verification: When (and Why Not) to Trust Symbolic Constraints in LLM Reasoning"

三层结构：
1. **诊断层**: 量化两个根本瓶颈 — independence bottleneck (κ=0.21-0.82, Eff-K=1.2-3.6) + symbolic confirmation bias (Bias Ratio=4.97×, 错误题上 constraints 系统性确认错误答案)
2. **因果链层**: 解释为什么 12+ 种干预全部失败 — 不是方法不对，而是因果链: same model → correlated traces → biased FOL extraction → confirmation bias amplification。打断任何一环的尝试（换模型提取、contrastive prompting、debate、温度调节等）都未能成功
3. **选择性解决层**: 不是"symbolic verification 无效"，而是"在特定 regime 有效"。Goldilocks zone (SC~50-56%, κ~0.45-0.50) 有 directional improvement (+2-3pp)；Selective Abstention (AUROC=0.673, +4.4pp@80% coverage) 提供实用诊断价值

**正在进行的新实验**（可能影响 framing）：
- Direction R (DeBERTa External Verifier): 用 encoder-only NLI 模型作为架构独立验证器，如果成功将为第三层提供更强的 positive result
- Direction F (Blind FOL): 从 premises-only 提取 FOL，打断 confirmation bias 因果链
- Direction H (Adversarial Negation): 强制生成反面 constraints

**请评估**：
1. 新叙事 vs 当前叙事：哪个更适合 EMNLP 2026？
2. 三层结构是否连贯？是否足以支撑 A 会 (Overall Assessment ≥ 4.0)？
3. 如果 R (DeBERTa) 成功，framing 该如何调整？如果 R 也失败呢？
4. 目前 12+ 失败干预 + 3 个 directional positive + entropy decomposition regime analysis 能否撑起 8 页 main conference paper？
5. 哪些实验结果是当前叙事的 gap？

不需要写论文，只需要评估 framing 可行性并提出建议。引用不需要验证。

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
The Director is asking me to evaluate a new proposed framing "Selective Symbolic Verification" against the current framing. This is a framing evaluation task, not a writing task. I need to analyze this carefully based on my deep knowledge of the paper's data, experiments, and the EMNLP 2026 standards.

Let me think through each question systematically.

First, let me understand the two framings:

**Current framing (D085)**: "Comprehensive negative result + diagnostic analysis" — Independence bottleneck is structural, universal across architectures, rooted in Symbolic Confirmation Bias. 

The core finding is that 25+ interventions all fail, and Process-κ reveals a deeper bottleneck underneath. The paper essentially demonstrates that this whole approach doesn't work and explains why.

**Proposed new framing**: "Selective Symbolic Verification: When (and Why Not) to Trust Symbolic Constraints" — Three layers: diagnosis, causal chain, selective solution. This reframes from "nothing works" to "we know when it works and when it doesn't."

Let me evaluate:

### 1. New vs Current Framing for EMNLP 2026

**Current framing strengths:**
- Comprehensive negative result papers are valued at *ACL venues
- 25+ failed interventions is extremely thorough
- Confirmation bias + process-κ provide mechanistic insight
- Clear narrative: bottleneck is structural, here's proof
- Strong statistical evidence (0 significant positives, multiple significant negatives)

**Current framing weaknesses:**
- "Everything fails" can feel nihilistic to reviewers
- Actionable takeaways are limited (diagnostics are useful but minor)
- The 25+ failures may feel repetitive without a strong positive counterpoint
- Reviewer concern: "So what? We already knew self-consistency has limits."

**New framing strengths:**
- "When and Why Not" is more actionable and reviewer-friendly
- Three-layer structure gives the paper a clearer arc (problem → mechanism → solution)
- Selective abstention (+4.4pp@80% coverage) becomes a genuine contribution
- Goldilocks zone becomes a feature, not a footnote
- Title is more engaging and less defeatist

**New framing weaknesses:**
- The "selective solution layer" is weak — it's only a modest improvement with significant coverage tradeoffs, so I need to be careful not to oversell it as a major contribution.

The data really does point toward a negative result, and trying to force a positive narrative around weak solutions risks looking like I'm overclaiming. EMNLP values negative results and analysis, so leaning into what the evidence actually shows is probably the stronger move here. much stronger evidence to be convincing (e.g., if DeBERTa works, THEN the framing shift makes sense).
4. Confirmation bias (4.97×) and process-κ are genuine novel contributions that shine in an analysis framing.

However, the new framing has one good insight: the current framing could benefit from a clearer "so what" / actionable takeaway. The diagnostics (C0+SC, Eff-K) ARE actionable but currently feel like an afterthought.

### 2. Three-layer structure coherence

Layer 1 (Diagnosis): Strong. κ quantification + confirmation bias are well-supported.
Layer 2 (Causal chain): Strong. 25+ failures + causal chain is compelling.
Layer 3 (Selective solution): WEAK. The evidence doesn't support it. The Goldilocks zone is narrow and model-specific, selective abstention only achieves 0.673 AUROC (barely above random), and the +4.4pp improvement only holds at 80% coverage. There's no evidence this selective approach generalizes beyond the current setup.

For a ≥4.0 paper, all three layers need to be strong, and Layer 3 is the weakest link that could undermine the whole contribution. Reviewers will zero in on this.

### 3. If DeBERTa succeeds

If DeBERTa works, it fundamentally reshapes the narrative: external verification becomes the proof that an architecture-independent signal can break through the bottleneck, which would justify the selective symbolic verification framing and elevate Layer 3 from weak to strong. If it fails, I need to reconsider the entire approach.

### 4. Can current evidence support 8pp main conference?

The content is there—25+ failures, confirmation bias quantification, process-κ, oracle gap, independence-accuracy trade-off—and the D085 framing has a clean narrative arc. The real question is whether it reaches ≥4.0, and my assessment is that the novel contributions (confirmation bias quantification, process-κ metric, independence-accuracy trade-off, exhaustive failure analysis across 6 models and 3 domains) are strong enough to carry it, but Layer 3 remains the vulnerability.

### 5. Gaps in current narrative

I'm noticing several loose threads that could undermine the story: the confirmation bias overlap discrepancy between 94% and 97.6%, process-κ only validated on Mistral-7B FOLIO, the Direction F blind FOL results that might break the causal chain, all interventions tested at 7B scale only, and the Selective Abstention AUROC of 0.673 lacking proper context. These need resolution before submission.

Looking at the cross-model SICA results more carefully — phase 1 shows cross-model independence is actually higher (kappa_cross=0.422 vs kappa_within=0.532), but phase 2 reveals this independence doesn't translate to accuracy gains (Δ=-0.49pp). That's the real tension here: independence and accuracy don't move together, which is a crucial finding about the independence-accuracy trade-off that I'm underutilizing in the narrative.

I also need to flag a discrepancy — the Director references "12+ 失败干预" but the actual count is 25+ interventions across prompts, methods, and waves. The writing plan I generated uses the older framing, so if the Director approves the new one, I'll need to update it. And since Directions R, F, and H are still in progress, the plan should remain flexible enough to incorporate their results once they're available.
```

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Now I have all the data I need. Let me write a comprehensive framing evaluation. Let me be thorough but focused on the 5 questions the Director asked.

Key data points confirmed:
- exp-confirmation-bias: bias_ratio=4.97, wrong_constraints=83.3%, sica_errors_follow_sc=94% (NOT 97.6% as framing says)
- exp-direction-e-abstention: AUROC=0.673, +4.4pp@80% coverage, +8.8pp@60% coverage


- exp-cross-model-phase1: kappa_cross=0.422 vs kappa_within=0.532, accuracy_gain=0
- exp-cross-model-phase2: delta=-0.49pp
- 25+ interventions all negative (confirmed across registry)

Now evaluating the new "Selective Symbolic Verification" framing against the Director's questions. The fundamental issue with Layer 3 (selective solution) is that it lacks empirical support—the Goldilocks zone only appears in Mistral-7B on FOLIO with a marginal +3.43pp improvement that doesn't reach statistical significance.

Selective abstention shows weak discriminative power at 0.673 AUROC, barely above random, and the gains come at the cost of discarding substantial portions of the test set. The current D085 framing is more defensible because it acknowledges the negative results directly rather than attempting to reframe them as evidence for a solution that the data doesn't actually support.

What makes this approach stronger is that negative results are genuinely valued at ACL venues, and the diagnostic contributions—the metrics around κ, effective K, confirmation bias patterns—are substantive and useful even without a working method. The scale of the study across 25+ interventions, 6 models, and 3 domains gives it real weight as a definitive investigation. The new framing does have merit in making those diagnostics more prominent rather than burying them, but rebranding as "Selective Symbolic Verification" is misleading since the title suggests the method actually works. Better to keep the honest D085 framing while elevating how the diagnostic insights are presented.

Looking at the three-layer coherence, the diagnosis and causal chain are solid—the κ quantification, confirmation bias findings, and the 25+ failure cases form a strong backbone. But the selective solution layer is the weak point. The Goldilocks results don't reach significance, abstention only gets 0.673 AUROC, and everything is confined to one model and dataset. For a 4.0+ at EMNLP, that third layer would need to show something genuinely generalizable, otherwise a reviewer will rightfully push back on whether a +4.4pp gain on 80% of questions for just Mistral-7B on FOLIO actually matters.

The three-layer structure itself creates an implicit promise of resolution that the current results can't fulfill, which sets up reviewer disappointment. But if DeBERTa works—using an external encoder-only model for verification—that changes everything. It would prove the self-extraction bottleneck can be broken with outside verification, deliver a real positive result for Layer 3, and make the whole "Selective Symbolic Verification" framing actually land. That could push the paper from 3.5 to 4.0-4.5. If it fails, it just adds another data point to the growing pile of failures and strengthens the negative framing instead.

For the main conference submission, the D085 analysis framing is solid enough for 8 pages. The novelty is there—quantifying confirmation bias at 4.97×, the process-κ metric, the independence-accuracy trade-off Pareto frontier—plus the sheer thoroughness of 25+ interventions is genuinely unprecedented. The Condorcet framework with Eff-K provides theoretical grounding, the oracle control cleanly isolates causality, and testing across three architecture families confirms this isn't a one-off quirk. The main risk is a reviewer dismissing it as obvious since LLMs having biases is already known, but no prior work has actually quantified it this way.

I'm estimating 3.5-4.0 overall. The confirmation bias findings and process-κ trends push toward 4.0, and the exhaustive nature of the 25+ failed interventions gives it "definitive study" weight. But the complete absence of even a marginal positive result might anchor some reviewers at 3.5.

Before writing though, I need to resolve two gaps: the confirmation bias overlap between registry and framing conditions is suspiciously high (94% vs 97.6%), and the process-κ metric is only validated on Mistral-7B with FOLIO. Cross-model validation would significantly strengthen that claim.

The remaining experiments matter for the causal narrative. Direction F tests whether blind FOL breaks the answer-to-FOL chain—either outcome clarifies whether the bias lives in the traces themselves. Direction H with adversarial negation shows whether forcing counter-arguments helps. And the cross-model SICA results are underutilized evidence: the κ drop from 0.532 to 0.422 combined with zero accuracy gains directly demonstrates that even moderate independence improvements don't break the bottleneck.

The selective abstention generalization only validated on Mistral-7B FOLIO, so I need cross-model confirmation before framing it as diagnostic. Larger 70B+ models would reveal whether the trade-off shifts with scale—reviewers will definitely ask about that.
```

{
  "text": "[Framing 评估] \"Selective Symbolic Verification\" vs 当前 D085 叙事\n\n## 1. 新叙事 vs 当前叙事：哪个更适合 EMNLP 2026？\n\n**结论：当前 D085 framing 更强，但可吸收新叙事的\"诊断实用性\"角度。**\n\n核心判断依据——数据方向与叙事方向的一致性：\n\n当前 D085 的数据全部指向 negative result：\n- 0/4 seeds significant positive（best p=0.092）\n- 唯一 significant effects 全部 negative（PW -2.83pp p=0.014, -2.50pp p=0.011, Debate -18.6pp p<0.001）\n- 25+ interventions ALL fail\n- confirmation bias ratio 4.97×（数据明确指向\"不行\"）\n\n新叙事 Layer 3（\"选择性解决\"）试图从这些数据中挤出 positive story：\n- Goldilocks zone +2-3pp：**0/4 seeds significant**，只在 Mistral-7B × FOLIO 出现\n- Selective Abstention AUROC=0.673：**勉强高于 random**（0.5 baseline），+4.4pp 代价是丢弃 20% test set\n- 这不构成\"解决层\"，reviewer 会直接攻击\n\n**风险矩阵**：\n| 叙事 | 最强 claim | 最弱 claim | Reviewer 攻击面 |\n|------|-----------|-----------|---------------|\n| D085 (negative) | 25+ failures + confirmation bias 4.97× + process-κ | \"So what?\" / actionable value 有限 | 小：数据完美支撑叙事 |\n| Selective (positive spin) | 三层结构叙事完整 | Layer 3 无 significant positive | 大：overclaiming vs data |\n\n**EMNLP 2026 venue fit**：*ACL 系统明确接受 well-executed negative results。Findings track 本身就为此设计。D085 叙事自然瞄准 3.5-4.0（Main/Findings 边界），风险低。新叙事如果 Layer 3 被击穿，整个三层结构坍塌，可能降到 3.0 以下。\n\n## 2. 三层结构连贯性\n\n- **Layer 1（诊断）**: ✅ 强。κ=0.21-0.82 + confirmation bias 4.97× 有坚实 registry 数据支撑。\n- **Layer 2（因果链）**: ✅ 强。25+ interventions 覆盖 8 个维度全部失败，因果链清晰。\n- **Layer 3（选择性解决）**: ❌ 弱。具体问题：\n  - Goldilocks zone：SC~50-56% 太窄（仅 Mistral-7B FOLIO），0/4 significant，不可推荐为实用方案\n  - Selective Abstention：AUROC 0.673 在 NLP 社区标准下偏低；+4.4pp@80% coverage 意味着放弃 20% 问题只换来微弱提升\n  - 两者都是\"directional observation\"级别，不是\"solution\"级别\n  - **致命问题**：如果标题是\"When (and Why Not) to Trust\"，reviewer 期望看到 clear when-to-trust recipe。数据不支撑。\n\n**能否撑 ≥4.0？** Layer 1+2 单独可以（这就是 D085 做的事）。加上 Layer 3 反而可能降分——reviewer 会认为 overclaiming。\n\n## 3. Direction R (DeBERTa) 的影响\n\n**如果 R 成功**（external verifier 显著提升）：\n- **Game changer**。论文叙事可以变为：\"same-model self-extraction fails (here's why) → external architecturally-independent verification succeeds (here's proof) → prescriptive guidance for practitioners\"\n- 此时\"Selective Symbolic Verification\"标题成立：Layer 3 有了 significant positive\n- 贡献升级：从纯 analysis → analysis + actionable solution\n- 预估评分：4.0-4.5（如果 DeBERTa 效果显著）\n- **建议**：R 成功后立即 reframe。此时 framing 变为 \"Why Self-Extracted Constraints Fail and How External Verification Fixes It\"\n\n**如果 R 也失败**：\n- 进一步强化 D085：26+ interventions fail，连架构独立的 external verifier 也不行\n- Layer 3 更加不可能\n- 坚持 D085 是唯一正确选择\n\n**建议：等 R 结果再做 framing 决策。** DDL May 25 还有 5 天。R 结果预期什么时候？\n\n## 4. 当前 evidence 够不够撑 8 页 main conference？\n\n**够。** 内容量不是问题，叙事密度高：\n\n| 贡献 | 强度 | 页面需求 |\n|------|------|---------|\n| Independence-accuracy trade-off | ★★★★ (significant, multi-method) | 1.0pp (analysis §5.5) |\n| Symbolic Confirmation Bias | ★★★★ (4.97×, causal chain) | 0.5pp (analysis §5.2) |\n| Process-κ | ★★★ (novel metric, single model) | 0.3pp (method + analysis) |\n| 25+ exhaustive failures | ★★★★ (comprehensiveness) | 0.5pp (compressed + appendix) |\n| Oracle gap 24% | ★★★★ (p<0.001, clean causal) | 0.3pp (experiments) |\n| Regime + diagnostics | ★★★ (systematic but 0 significant) | 0.3pp (experiments) |\n| Total | | ~2.9pp analysis+results |\n\n加上 Introduction 1.35pp + Method 1.2pp + Related Work 0.7pp + Discussion 0.4pp + Conclusion 0.25pp = 7.8pp。Display items ~0.6pp。刚好 8pp。\n\n**关键问题不是\"够不够\"而是\"能不能到 4.0\"**。我的评估：\n- D085 framing：**3.5-4.0**。Thoroughness 和 novelty（confirmation bias + process-κ）是亮点；缺少 actionable positive 是 ceiling。\n- Selective framing：**3.0-3.5**。Layer 3 overclaiming 风险拉低。\n- D085 + DeBERTa 成功：**4.0-4.5**。Analysis + solution = 完整故事。\n\n## 5. 当前叙事的 Gaps\n\n**Critical（影响叙事）**：\n1. **Confirmation bias overlap 数字冲突**：registry 显示 `sica_errors_follow_sc = 0.94`（94%），framing 写 97.6%。差异 3.6pp 需要追溯实验 output 确认。**写入论文前必须解决。**\n2. **Process-κ 仅 Mistral-7B FOLIO 单条件**：作为\"new metric\"contribution 需要至少 1 个跨模型验证点（Qwen 或 LLaMA 的 cosine similarity）。否则 reviewer 会质疑 generalizability。\n\n**Important（影响 robustness）**：\n3. **Cross-model SICA (exp-cross-model-phase2) 数据未充分利用**：κ_cross=0.422 < κ_within=0.532（更独立）但 Δ=-0.49pp（不 help）。这是 independence-accuracy trade-off 的直接证据——跨模型增加了独立性但没有提升准确率。应在 §5.5 trade-off 讨论中引用。\n4. **Direction F/H 结果缺失**：Blind FOL 直接测试 confirmation bias 因果链（从 premises-only 提取，打断\"trace→FOL 确认答案\"链条）。如果 F 成功，confirmation bias claim 需要 nuance；如果 F 失败，进一步强化。**结果何时 ready？**\n\n**Nice-to-have**：\n5. **Selective Abstention 在叙事中的定位**：AUROC=0.673 不够强作为 contribution，但作为 diagnostic tool 有价值。D085 framing 中建议放在 discussion \"paths forward\" 或 appendix，不做 main contribution。\n6. **Scale caveat**：所有 method interventions 在 7B。D085 已经 acknowledge 这点。\n\n## 总结建议\n\n1. **保持 D085 framing**，不切换到\"Selective Symbolic Verification\"\n2. **等 Direction R (DeBERTa) 结果**：成功→reframe 为 \"diagnosis + solution\"；失败→D085 更强\n3. **补 process-κ 跨模型验证**（至少 1 个额外模型的 cosine similarity）\n4. **解决 94% vs 97.6% 数字冲突**\n5. **利用 cross-model SICA (exp-cross-model-phase2) 数据**强化 trade-off 论证\n6. 如果时间允许：Direction F 结果纳入，无论正负都有价值\n\n需要决策：\n- R 和 F 的预期完成时间？如果在 May 23 前有结果可以纳入；May 24 就来不及了。\n- Process-κ 跨模型实验是否启动？（1 个额外模型，预计 2-3 小时）\n- 是否批准当前 **现在请做**:
1. 继续完善 outline（章节结构、每章 key points）

- 不要 commit LaTeX 正文内容

**等待信号**: 我会在 R/S 结果出来后通知你最终叙事方向，届时再全力写正文。预计 5/21 上午给你最终方向。

[Director 指令] ## writing_plan.yaml v2 审批：✅ 批准，附 3 项修正

### 决策回复

1. **Confirmation bias overlap 数字**: 用 registry 的 **94%**（sica_errors_follow_sc=0.94）为主。framing 的 97.6% 可能是不同方向的统计（SC→SICA vs SICA→SC），Agent 原始报告未区分。论文中使用 registry 数据，写作时表述为 "94% of SICA errors coincide with SC errors"。如果两个方向的数字都有意义，appendix 可补充双向数据。**不使用未经 registry 确认的数字。**

2. **立即开始写作**: 批准。从 method（§3.5 process-κ 定义）开始。

3. **Direction F (Blind FOL)**: **不等**。Reviewer 报告早期信号：2/204 全部预测 Unknown（blind extraction 缺少待验证命题 → Z3 无法判定 entailment）。预期最终 accuracy ≈ Unknown 占比 (~20%) << SC baseline (54%)。这是强 negative，符合预期但结果太差不值得等。如果最终结果有 surprise，Patch 补入 appendix 作为额外证据。

### 3 项修正要求

**M1: "25+ interventions" 计数必须在 appendix 中逐条列出。** Reviewer 审查要求论文中的数字经得起审稿人核实。Wave 3 未完成的方向（F/G/H/K/L）**不计入**"all failed"——它们是 "attempted but not completed"，论文中不引用其结果。实际完成且 negative 的干预约 **22 种**，请在 appendix 用 table 逐条列出（method name + 1-line description + result + 1-line failure mode）。正文可用 "more than 20 interventions" 或精确数字。

**M2: Contributions 排序确认。** C1 (Independence-Accuracy Trade-off) 作为 PRIMARY 正确——这是最 fundamental 的发现。C2 (Confirmation Bias) 作为 CORE 也正确——这是 mechanism explanation。保持当前排序。

**M3: Page budget 的 -0.25pp 安全边际太薄。** EMNLP 严格 8 页。如果写作过程中发现超页，优先压缩 Discussion（当前 framing 中 regime 分析可以部分移入 appendix）。绝不压缩 Analysis（那是论文核心）。

### 写作节奏期望

- May 20-21: method + experiments + analysis 完成
- May 22: related_work + introduction + discussion + abstract + conclusion 完成
- May 23: 初稿完整 → 提交 Review
- 每完成一个 major section 汇报一次进展

开始吧。

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_019xhPAXxuPdAvG3U7nrwfQt`</summary>

````text
1	---
2	name: paper-writing-norms
3	description: 顶会级别论文写作风格规范——章节写法（Abstract/Intro/Related Work）、去 AI 风格、视觉密度、叙事哲学。Paper Agent 和 Worker 写论文章节时必须加载。
4	allowed-tools: [Read, Write, Edit, Glob, Grep]
5	scope: global
6	version: 2
7	priority: 9
8	tags:
9	  - writing
10	  - paper
11	  - style
12	  - norms
13	roles:
14	  - agent
15	  - worker
16	  - paper_agent
17	---
18	
19	# 论文写作风格规范
20	
21	> 核心哲学：**论文是一个有说服力的故事，不是实验报告。** 审稿人在几十秒内判断论文价值——如果 Abstract 和 Introduction 读起来像实验日志，论文就已经失败了。
22	
23	---
24	
25	## 一、总体写作哲学
26	
27	### 1.1 故事驱动，不是数据堆砌
28	
29	- 论文必须以"故事线"为核心展开：问题 → 洞察 → 方法 → 验证
30	- 禁止大量实验数据堆砌——每个数字出现必须服务于叙事推进
31	- 附录内容必须在正文中有明确引用且融入叙事，禁止简单堆砌
32	- 如果附录数据对核心叙事有重大影响，根据附录数据调整论文 framing
33	
34	### 1.2 主线统一性
35	
36	- 当 idea 在研究过程中发生变化后，**必须重审全文逻辑**
37	- 常见问题：Introduction 讲的故事 A，Method 做的是故事 B，Results 又在讲故事 C
38	- 修复方式：从最终实验结论反推，重写 Introduction 的问题定义和动机
39	- 每次 idea 调整后检查：Abstract → Introduction → Method → Results → Discussion → Conclusion 是否形成一条连贯主线
40	
41	### 1.3 专有名词必须有定义
42	
43	- 首次出现的专有名词/缩写必须给出完整定义和解释
44	- Abstract 中使用的缩写也必须在 Abstract 内定义（如 LLM = Large Language Model）
45	- 不能假设读者知道你自己定义的术语——论文从来没解释过的概念不能直接使用
46	- 通用规则：如果一个非标准术语第一次出现时没有括号定义，就是写作 bug
47	
48	### 1.4 写作前必读同类优秀论文
49	
50	- 每个章节动笔前，找 **2-3 篇目标会议近 1-2 年的高分论文**作为风格参考——同类问题或同类方法皆可
51	- 重点学：
52	  - Abstract 怎么用最少篇幅讲完故事
53	  - Introduction 怎么从大问题切入到具体贡献
54	  - Related Work 怎么 positioning 自己（不是 survey）
55	  - Table/Figure 怎么压缩信息密度
56	- **不是抄袭**，是学习同行的故事节奏和审美——不同子领域叙事节奏差异很大
57	- 实操：Paper Agent 可以从 `literature_map.yaml` 中挑 `role=foundation` 或 `role=direct-competitor` 的论文，把 `{title, venue, year, abstract}` 附在 Worker 的 task_prompt 中作为概念参考；如需学习写作风格本身，让 Worker 通过 WebSearch 取得论文链接并 fetch 全文
58	
59	---
60	
61	## 二、Abstract 写作规范
62	
63	### 2.1 四个必答问题（150-250 words）
64	
65	Abstract 必须在很短的篇幅内清楚回答：
66	1. **研究什么问题** — 1-2 句话交代背景和痛点
67	2. **为什么重要** — 现有方法的不足（不用写太多）
68	3. **本文提出什么** — 核心贡献的关键机制（简要，不堆砌细节）
69	4. **结果说明什么** — 用具体实验结论支撑贡献
70	
71	### 2.2 禁忌
72	
73	- **禁止堆砌数据**：Abstract 不是 Results 的缩写。最多引用 2-3 个关键数字，要服务于 claim
74	- **禁止过度宣传**：不说 "revolutionary"、"groundbreaking"
75	- **禁止写成 Introduction 的缩写**：Abstract 是独立阅读单元
76	- **禁止术语堆积**：如果审稿人几十秒内判断不出价值，就失败了
77	- **缩写必须定义**：Abstract 中首次出现的缩写必须展开
78	
79	### 2.3 审稿人视角检验
80	
81	写完后用这个标准自检：审稿人能否在 30 秒内判断——
82	- 问题是否重要？
83	- 方法是否清楚？
84	- 贡献是否可信？
85	- 结果是否有说服力？
86	
87	---
88	
89	## 三、Introduction 写作规范
90	
91	### 3.1 完整叙事线（5 段结构）
92	
93	Introduction 需要从一个清晰、重要的问题切入，逐步把读者带到核心贡献：
94	
95	1. **研究背景**（1-2 段）：大模型在某类能力/应用中已取得进展，但仍存在一个关键瓶颈
96	2. **问题重要性**（1 段）：结合真实应用、已有方法局限或当前趋势，说明这不是小问题
97	3. **现有方法不足**（1 段）：系统梳理为什么不够（依赖更大模型/更多数据/更复杂推理/更高成本，或只在特定 benchmark 有效）
98	4. **本文核心想法**（1 段）：我们观察到什么、提出了什么方法、为什么能解决上述问题
99	5. **贡献总结**（列表）：清楚列出方法贡献、实验贡献和分析贡献
100	
101	### 3.2 整体叙事结构
102	
103	```
104	现有进展 → 关键缺口 → 本文洞察 → 方法概述 → 实验证据 → 贡献总结
105	```
106	
107	### 3.3 禁忌
108	
109	- **禁止泛泛介绍**：不要开头就写 "LLMs have shown remarkable capabilities..."，要从具体问题切入
110	- **禁止缺少过渡**：每段之间必须有逻辑连接
111	- **禁止实验报告风格**：Introduction 不是 "我们做了 X 实验得到了 Y 结果" 的罗列
112	- 每条 Contribution 必须有对应的 section/table 支撑
113	- Contribution 列表中不能出现未在前文铺垫过的概念
114	
115	---
116	
117	## 四、Related Work 写作规范
118	
119	### 4.1 目标
120	
121	让审稿人相信：作者理解这个领域，知道自己的方法站在什么位置，清楚相对已有工作的真实增量在哪里。
122	
123	### 4.2 结构要求
124	
125	- 把已有工作分成几个清晰的主题（如模型训练、推理增强、工具调用、数据构建、评测等）
126	- 每类简要说明：已有方法做了什么、解决了哪些问题、和本文的关系
127	- 帮助读者理解：哪些是本文直接继承的，哪些方法不同但解决相似问题，哪些留下了本文要解决的缺口
128	
129	### 4.3 禁忌
130	
131	- **禁止论文堆砌**：不要写成 "A proposed X. B proposed Y. C proposed Z." 的列表
132	- **目标不是展示读了多少论文**，而是做归纳和比较
133	- Contribution claim（"To our knowledge, no prior work..."）应放在 Introduction，不在 Related Work
134	
135	### 4.4 引用充足度
136	
137	- 引用必须覆盖：**直接相关方法、baseline 论文、评测 benchmark 论文、理论基础**
138	- 任何一类缺失，审稿人会立刻看出来调研不充分
139	- 引用密度参照目标会议近年录用论文的量级——明显偏少意味着 Related Work 调研不足
140	
141	---
142	
143	## 五、去 AI 写作风格（硬规则）
144	
145	以下模式是 AI 生成文本的典型标志，审稿人一眼能看出。**必须全部消除。**
146	
147	### 5.1 严禁破折号（最显著的 AI 痕迹）
148	
149	**这是 AI 写作最容易被识别的标志，审稿人最敏感。单列为最高优先级硬规则。**
150	
151	- 解释性 em-dash（—，U+2014）和 en-dash（–，U+2013）必须**全部删除**
152	- 替代方式：用逗号、括号或拆分为两个句子
153	- 强制检查命令：
154	  ```bash
155	  grep -n "—" paper/*.tex     # em-dash，每处人工判断是否必要
156	  grep -n " – " paper/*.tex   # 解释性 en-dash，几乎都应删除
157	  ```
158	- 例外（允许保留）：表格分隔符、数字范围（`2020–2024`、`pp. 12–18`）、专有名词（`Newton–Raphson`）
159	
160	### 5.2 段落与句式
161	
162	- **禁止过度分段**：不需要每 2-3 句就换段，自然段 5-8 句是正常的
163	- **减少关系从句**（which, that, who）：能用简洁直接的句子就不用从句
164	- **禁止 -ing 短语结尾**：如 "..., highlighting the applicability of..." / "..., demonstrating the effectiveness of..."。这是 AI 写作最典型的标志之一
165	- **禁止连续使用 "Furthermore" / "Moreover" / "Additionally"**：一段内最多用一个过渡词
166	- **避免 "It is worth noting that..." / "Notably, ..."**：直接写要说的内容
167	
168	### 5.3 用词黑名单
169	
170	- 避免 "delve into" / "leverage" / "utilize" / "facilitate" — 用 "explore" / "use" / "enable"
171	- 避免 "a myriad of" / "a plethora of" — 用 "many" / "various"
172	- 避免 "in the realm of" / "in the context of" — 直接说
173	- 避免 "paradigm shift" / "novel" 过度使用
174	
175	### 5.4 校对清单（4 步）
176	
177	1. 全文 proofread 语法和拼写
178	2. 检查所有代词/指示词（this, that, it）是否指代明确
179	3. **`grep "—"` + `grep " – "` 全文清零**（删除所有解释性 dash，§5.1）
180	4. `grep "ing,"` 模式——大部分 -ing 分词短语需要改写
181	
182	---
183	
184	## 六、图表与视觉规范
185	
186	### 6.1 知识密度原则
187	
188	- **图表的信息必须紧凑**——不能为了美观而留大量白空间
189	- 一张图如果只传达一个简单信息，要么缩小要么合并到其他图中
190	- 能用 inline text 说清的不要占一整个 Figure
191	
192	### 6.2 表格规范
193	
194	- 表格和正文文字不能视觉重叠（检查 column 宽度和 alignment）
195	- 小数精度全表统一
196	- 最优加粗、次优下划线
197	- Caption 必须自包含
198	
199	### 6.3 图片规范
200	
201	- 数值标签不能被线、bar、点遮住——用白色背景或偏移标签
202	- 图中字体 >= 7pt（打印后仍可读）
203	- 栏宽约束需匹配目标 venue 模板要求
204	- 概念图/流程图要有信息量，不要画一个 trivial 的 pipeline 然后占半页
205	
206	### 6.4 空间效率
207	
208	- 顶会正文页数严格受限（具体页数查目标会议官网），每 cm² 都是宝贵资源
209	- 优先使用 `\subfigure` 合并相关图
210	- Table 能 inline 就不要单独浮动
211	- 图表占比与其对叙事的贡献成正比
212	
213	### 6.5 页面节奏
214	
215	好论文的页面有节奏感——text、equation、figure 交替出现，读者视觉不疲劳：
216	
217	- **text-equation-figure 三重奏**：重要概念用"文字解释 → 公式定义 → 图表直觉"三步展开
218	- 连续 2 页纯文本（无 figure/equation/table）→ 穿插一个 display item 打破节奏
219	- 连续 2 个 figure 页（整页被 float 占满）→ 至少隔 1 段文字再放下一个 float
220	- Section heading 不孤悬页底（heading 后至少跟 2 行正文，否则强制分页）
221	- 最后一页内容 ≥ 2/3 页面高度（太空显得凑数，太满放不下 references）
222	
223	### 6.6 表格 LaTeX 规范
224	
225	**Booktabs 标准（硬规则）**：
226	- 用 `\toprule` / `\midrule` / `\bottomrule`，禁止 `\hline`，禁止竖线 `|`
227	- 用 `\centering` 不用 `\begin{center}`（后者多加垂直空白）
228	- 最优结果加粗（`\textbf{}`），次优下划线（`\underline{}`）+ 方向箭头 `↑`/`↓` 标明指标方向
229	- 小数精度全表统一（如全部保留 1 位或 2 位）
230	- Caption 在表格上方（`\caption` 在 `\begin{tabular}` 之前）
231	
232	**宽度决策树**：
233	- ≤5 列 → 单栏宽度（`\columnwidth`）
234	- 6–8 列 → 全文宽度（`\textwidth`，双栏模板用 `table*`）
235	- >8 列 → 拆成两张表或移入 appendix
236	
237	---
238	
239	## 七、投稿前通用准备
240	
241	### 7.1 模板必须官方（硬规则）
242	
243	- LaTeX 模板**必须从目标会议官网下载**（CallForPapers / Author Instructions 页面）
244	- **不要用**：
245	  - 网上他人转载的复制版本（可能被改过 margin / 行号设置）
246	  - 上一年的模板（参数可能变：字体、margin、行号、anonymous 选项等）
247	  - 组里学长留的旧版本（来源不明、无法追溯）
248	- 风险：
249	  - 样式不符合 → desk reject
250	  - margin 不对 → 被审稿人质疑诚信
251	  - reference 格式错 → 被认为不规范
252	- 流程：先查会议官网 templates → 找不到再 `WebSearch "{venue} {year} latex template official"` → 仍找不到汇报 Director（不要用就近年版本顶替）
253	- `\documentclass[]{}` 选项必须与目标 venue + 阶段（review / camera-ready）一致
254	
255	### 7.2 投稿要求逐项核对
256	
257	- 目标会议如要求 Checklist / Ethical Statement / Broader Impact Statement / Reproducibility Statement，必须**认真填每一项**，不要全写 N/A
258	- 页数限制：查目标会议官网
259	- 匿名化要求：review 版 vs camera-ready 版本设置不同
260	- 行号开启（review 版常需）
261	- 字体大小、margin 不得修改 sty 文件
262	
263	### 7.3 最终修改 10 项检查流程
264	
265	提交前必须逐一检查：
266	1. 所有最新实验结果已全部录入
267	2. 根据实验结果将叙事调至最优状态
268	3. 附录所有内容在正文中被引用且融入叙事
269	4. 无数据堆砌（每个数字服务于叙事推进）
270	5. Abstract 和 Introduction 具有吸引力（不是实验报告风格）
271	6. 全文主线统一（idea 变化后各章节逻辑一致）
272	7. 所有专有名词有定义
273	8. 无 AI 风格标志（dash、-ing phrases、过度分段）
274	9. 图表知识密度高、无视觉遮挡
275	10. Reference 覆盖直接相关方法、baseline、benchmark、理论基础
276	
````

</details>

      "content": "---\nname: paper-writing-norms\ndescription: 顶会级别论文写作风格规范——章节写法（Abstract/Intro/Related Work）、去 AI 风格、视觉密度、叙事哲学。Paper Agent 和 Worker 写论文章节时必须加载。\nallowed-tools: [Read, Write, Edit, Glob, Grep]\nscope: global\nversion: 2\npriority: 9\ntags:\n  - writing\n  - paper\n  - style\n  - norms\nroles:\n  - agent\n  - worker\n  - paper_agent\n---\n\n# 论文写作风格规范\n\n> 核心哲学：**论文是一个有说服力的故事，不是实验报告。** 审稿人在几十秒内判断论文价值——如果 Abstract 和 Introduction 读起来像实验日志，论文就已经失败了。\n\n---\n\n## 一、总体写作哲学\n\n### 1.1 故事驱动，不是数据堆砌\n\n- 论文必须以\"故事线\"为核心展开：问题 → 洞察 → 方法 → 验证\n- 禁止大量实验数据堆砌——每个数字出现必须服务于叙事推进\n- 附录内容必须在正文中有明确引用且融入叙事，禁止简单堆砌\n- 如果附录数据对核心叙事有重大影响，根据附录数据调整论文 framing\n\n### 1.2 主线统一性\n\n- 当 idea 在研究过程中发生变化后，**必须重审全文逻辑**\n- 常见问题：Introduction 讲的故事 A，Method 做的是故事 B，Results 又在讲故事 C\n- 修复方式：从最终实验结论反推，重写 Introduction 的问题定义和动机\n- 每次 idea 调整后检查：Abstract → Introduction → Method → Results → Discussion → Conclusion 是否形成一条连贯主线\n\n### 1.3 专有名词必须有定义\n\n- 首次出现的专有名词/缩写必须给出完整定义和解释\n- Abstract 中使用的缩写也必须在 Abstract 内定义（如 LLM = Large Language Model）\n- 不能假设读者知道你自己定义的术语——论文从来没解释过的概念不能直接使用\n- 通用规则：如果一个非标准术语第一次出现时没有括号定义，就是写作 bug\n\n### 1.4 写作前必读同类优秀论文\n\n- 每个章节动笔前，找 **2-3 篇目标会议近 1-2 年的高分论文**作为风格参考——同类问题或同类方法皆可\n- 重点学：\n  - Abstract 怎么用最少篇幅讲完故事\n  - Introduction 怎么从大问题切入到具体贡献\n  - Related Work 怎么 positioning 自己（不是 survey）\n  - Table/Figure 怎么压缩信息密度\n- **不是抄袭**，是学习同行的故事节奏和审美——不同子领域叙事节奏差异很大\n- 实操：Paper Agent 可以从 `literature_map.yaml` 中挑 `role=foundation` 或 `role=direct-competitor` 的论文，把 `{title, venue, year, abstract}` 附在 Worker 的 task_prompt 中作为概念参考；如需学习写作风格本身，让 Worker 通过 WebSearch 取得论文链接并 fetch 全文\n\n---\n\n## 二、Abstract 写作规范\n\n### 2.1 四个必答问题（150-250 words）\n\nAbstract 必须在很短的篇幅内清楚回答：\n1. **研究什么问题** — 1-2 句话交代背景和痛点\n2. **为什么重要** — 现有方法的不足（不用写太多）\n3. **本文提出什么** — 核心贡献的关键机制（简要，不堆砌细节）\n4. **结果说明什么** — 用具体实验结论支撑贡献\n\n### 2.2 禁忌\n\n- **禁止堆砌数据**：Abstract 不是 Results 的缩写。最多引用 2-3 个关键数字，要服务于 claim\n- **禁止过度宣传**：不说 \"revolutionary\"、\"groundbreaking\"\n- **禁止写成 Introduction 的缩写**：Abstract 是独立阅读单元\n- **禁止术语堆积**：如果审稿人几十秒内判断不出价值，就失败了\n- **缩写必须定义**：Abstract 中首次出现的缩写必须展开\n\n### 2.3 审稿人视角检验\n\n写完后用这个标准自检：审稿人能否在 30 秒内判断——\n- 问题是否重要？\n- 方法是否清楚？\n- 贡献是否可信？\n- 结果是否有说服力？\n\n---\n\n## 三、Introduction 写作规范\n\n### 3.1 完整叙事线（5 段结构）\n\nIntroduction 需要从一个清晰、重要的问题切入，逐步把读者带到核心贡献：\n\n1. **研究背景**（1-2 段）：大模型在某类能力/应用中已取得进展，但仍存在一个关键瓶颈\n2. **问题重要性**（1 段）：结合真实应用、已有方法局限或当前趋势，说明这不是小问题\n3. **现有方法不足**（1 段）：系统梳理为什么不够（依赖更大模型/更多数据/更复杂推理/更高成本，或只在特定 benchmark 有效）\n4. **本文核心想法**（1 段）：我们观察到什么、提出了什么方法、为什么能解决上述问题\n5. **贡献总结**（列表）：清楚列出方法贡献、实验贡献和分析贡献\n\n### 3.2 整体叙事结构\n\n```\n现有进展 → 关键缺口 → 本文洞察 → 方法概述 → 实验证据 → 贡献总结\n```\n\n### 3.3 禁忌\n\n- **禁止泛泛介绍**：不要开头就写 \"LLMs have shown remarkable capabilities...\"，要从具体问题切入\n- **禁止缺少过渡**：每段之间必须有逻辑连接\n- **禁止实验报告风格**：Introduction 不是 \"我们做了 X 实验得到了 Y 结果\" 的罗列\n- 每条 Contribution 必须有对应的 section/table 支撑\n- Contribution 列表中不能出现未在前文铺垫过的概念\n\n---\n\n## 四、Related Work 写作规范\n\n### 4.1 目标\n\n让审稿人相信：作者理解这个领域，知道自己的方法站在什么位置，清楚相对已有工作的真实增量在哪里。\n\n### 4.2 结构要求\n\n- 把已有工作分成几个清晰的主题（如模型训练、推理增强、工具调用、数据构建、评测等）\n- 每类简要说明：已有方法做了什么、解决了哪些问题、和本文的关系\n- 帮助读者理解：哪些是本文直接继承的，哪些方法不同但解决相似问题，哪些留下了本文要解决的缺口\n\n### 4.3 禁忌\n\n- **禁止论文堆砌**：不要写成 \"A proposed X. B proposed Y. C proposed Z.\" 的列表\n- **目标不是展示读了多少论文**，而是做归纳和比较\n- Contribution claim（\"To our knowledge, no prior work...\"）应放在 Introduction，不在 Related Work\n\n### 4.4 引用充足度\n\n- 引用必须覆盖：**直接相关方法、baseline 论文、评测 benchmark 论文、理论基础**\n- 任何一类缺失，审稿人会立刻看出来调研不充分\n- 引用密度参照目标会议近年录用论文的量级——明显偏少意味着 Related Work 调研不足\n\n---\n\n## 五、去 AI 写作风格（硬规则）\n\n以下模式是 AI 生成文本的典型标志，审稿人一眼能看出。**必须全部消除。**\n\n### 5.1 严禁破折号（最显著的 AI 痕迹）\n\n**这是 AI 写作最容易被识别的标志，审稿人最敏感。单列为最高优先级硬规则。**\n\n- 解释性 em-dash（—，U+2014）和 en-dash（–，U+2013）必须**全部删除**\n- 替代方式：用逗号、括号或拆分为两个句子\n- 强制检查命令：\n  ```bash\n  grep -n \"—\" paper/*.tex     # em-dash，每处人工判断是否必要\n  grep -n \" – \" paper/*.tex   # 解释性 en-dash，几乎都应删除\n  ```\n- 例外（允许保留）：表格分隔符、数字范围（`2020–2024`、`pp. 12–18`）、专有名词（`Newton–Raphson`）\n\n### 5.2 段落与句式\n\n- **禁止过度分段**：不需要每 2-3 句就换段，自然段 5-8 句是正常的\n- **减少关系从句**（which, that, who）：能用简洁直接的句子就不用从句\n- **禁止 -ing 短语结尾**：如 \"..., highlighting the applicability of...\" / \"..., demonstrating the effectiveness of...\"。这是 AI 写作最典型的标志之一\n- **禁止连续使用 \"Furthermore\" / \"Moreover\" / \"Additionally\"**：一段内最多用一个过渡词\n- **避免 \"It is worth noting that...\" / \"Notably, ...\"**：直接写要说的内容\n\n### 5.3 用词黑名单\n\n- 避免 \"delve into\" / \"leverage\" / \"utilize\" / \"facilitate\" — 用 \"explore\" / \"use\" / \"enable\"\n- 避免 \"a myriad of\" / \"a plethora of\" — 用 \"many\" / \"various\"\n- 避免 \"in the realm of\" / \"in the context of\" — 直接说\n- 避免 \"paradigm shift\" / \"novel\" 过度使用\n\n### 5.4 校对清单（4 步）\n\n1. 全文 proofread 语法和拼写\n2. 检查所有代词/指示词（this, that, it）是否指代明确\n3. **`grep \"—\"` + `grep \" – \"` 全文清零**（删除所有解释性 dash，§5.1）\n4. `grep \"ing,\"` 模式——大部分 -ing 分词短语需要改写\n\n---\n\n## 六、图表与视觉规范\n\n### 6.1 知识密度原则\n\n- **图表的信息必须紧凑**——不能为了美观而留大量白空间\n- 一张图如果只传达一个简单信息，要么缩小要么合并到其他图中\n- 能用 inline text 说清的不要占一整个 Figure\n\n### 6.2 表格规范\n\n- 表格和正文文字不能视觉重叠（检查 column 宽度和 alignment）\n- 小数精度全表统一\n- 最优加粗、次优下划线\n- Caption 必须自包含\n\n### 6.3 图片规范\n\n- 数值标签不能被线、bar、点遮住——用白色背景或偏移标签\n- 图中字体 >= 7pt（打印后仍可读）\n- 栏宽约束需匹配目标 venue 模板要求\n- 概念图/流程图要有信息量，不要画一个 trivial 的 pipeline 然后占半页\n\n### 6.4 空间效率\n\n- 顶会正文页数严格受限（具体页数查目标会议官网），每 cm² 都是宝贵资源\n- 优先使用 `\\subfigure` 合并相关图\n- Table 能 inline 就不要单独浮动\n- 图表占比与其对叙事的贡献成正比\n\n### 6.5 页面节奏\n\n好论文的页面有节奏感——text、equation、figure 交替出现，读者视觉不疲劳：\n\n- **text-equation-figure 三重奏**：重要概念用\"文字解释 → 公式定义 → 图表直觉\"三步展开\n- 连续 2 页纯文本（无 figure/equation/table）→ 穿插一个 display item 打破节奏\n- 连续 2 个 figure 页（整页被 float 占满）→ 至少隔 1 段文字再放下一个 float\n- Section heading 不孤悬页底（heading 后至少跟 2 行正文，否则强制分页）\n- 最后一页内容 ≥ 2/3 页面高度（太空显得凑数，太满放不下 references）\n\n### 6.6 表格 LaTeX 规范\n\n**Booktabs 标准（硬规则）**：\n- 用 `\\toprule` / `\\midrule` / `\\bottomrule`，禁止 `\\hline`，禁止竖线 `|`\n- 用 `\\centering` 不用 `\\begin{center}`（后者多加垂直空白）\n- 最优结果加粗（`\\textbf{}`），次优下划线（`\\underline{}`）+ 方向箭头 `↑`/`↓` 标明指标方向\n- 小数精度全表统一（如全部保留 1 位或 2 位）\n- Caption 在表格上方（`\\caption` 在 `\\begin{tabular}` 之前）\n\n**宽度决策树**：\n- ≤5 列 → 单栏宽度（`\\columnwidth`）\n- 6–8 列 → 全文宽度（`\\textwidth`，双栏模板用 `table*`）\n- >8 列 → 拆成两张表或移入 appendix\n\n---\n\n## 七、投稿前通用准备\n\n### 7.1 模板必须官方（硬规则）\n\n- LaTeX 模板**必须从目标会议官网下载**（CallForPapers / Author Instructions 页面）\n- **不要用**：\n  - 网上他人转载的复制版本（可能被改过 margin / 行号设置）\n  - 上一年的模板（参数可能变：字体、margin、行号、anonymous 选项等）\n  - 组里学长留的旧版本（来源不明、无法追溯）\n- 风险：\n  - 样式不符合 → desk reject\n  - margin 不对 → 被审稿人质疑诚信\n  - reference 格式错 → 被认为不规范\n- 流程：先查会议官网 templates → 找不到再 `WebSearch \"{venue} {year} latex template official\"` → 仍找不到汇报 Director（不要用就近年版本顶替）\n- `\\documentclass[]{}` 选项必须与目标 venue + 阶段（review / camera-ready）一致\n\n### 7.2 投稿要求逐项核对\n\n- 目标会议如要求 Checklist / Ethical Statement / Broader Impact Statement / Reproducibility Statement，必须**认真填每一项**，不要全写 N/A\n- 页数限制：查目标会议官网\n- 匿名化要求：review 版 vs camera-ready 版本设置不同\n- 行号开启（review 版常需）\n- 字体大小、margin 不得修改 sty 文件\n\n### 7.3 最终修改 10 项检查流程\n\n提交前必须逐一检查：\n1. 所有最新实验结果已全部录入\n2. 根据实验结果将叙事调至最优状态\n3. 附录所有内容在正文中被引用且融入叙事\n4. 无数据堆砌（每个数字服务于叙事推进）\n5. Abstract 和 Introduction 具有吸引力（不是实验报告风格）\n6. 全文主线统一（idea 变化后各章节逻辑一致）\n7. 所有专有名词有定义\n8. 无 AI 风格标志（dash、-ing phrases、过度分段）\n9. 图表知识密度高、无视觉遮挡\n10. Reference 覆盖直接相关方法、baseline、benchmark、理论基础\n",

    }

{
  "project": "symb_invariant_consensus",
  "section": "method",
  "old_text": "\\subsection{Scope}\n\\label{sec:scope}\n\nSICA uses \\emph{same-model self-extraction}: the same LLM generates traces and extracts constraints. We test twelve remediation strategies: nine prompt-level spanning six dimensions and three method-level (ICG, Contrastive, Debate) targeting the independence bottleneck (\\S\\ref{sec:remediation}). Methods using trained extractors, external provers, or multi-model ensembles fall outside scope.",
  "new_text": "\\subsection{Process-Level Independence}\n\\label{sec:process-kappa-method}\n\nAnswer-level Fleiss' $\\kappa$ may underestimate the true independence bottleneck. Two traces that follow nearly identical reasoning can reach different final answers; $\\kappa$ treats such pairs as disagreeing, overestimating independence. We quantify process-level similarity via pairwise cosine similarity over TF-IDF representations of chain-of-thought traces. For problem $q$ with $K$ traces:\n\\begin{equation}\n\\label{eq:process-sim}\n\\bar{s}(q) = \\frac{2}{K(K{-}1)} \\sum_{i < j} \\cos(\\mathbf{v}_i, \\mathbf{v}_j),\n\\end{equation}\nwhere $\\mathbf{v}_i$ is the TF-IDF vector of trace $t_i$. When $\\bar{s} \\gg \\kappa$, reasoning processes are more homogeneous than answers suggest. This metric applies to any trace aggregation method, not only SICA.\n\n\\subsection{Scope}\n\\label{sec:scope}\n\nSICA uses \\emph{same-model self-extraction}: the same LLM generates traces and extracts constraints. We test more than twenty remediation strategies: nine prompt-level spanning six dimensions, three method-level (ICG, Contrastive, Debate), and ten additional directions targeting the independence bottleneck from different angles (\\S\\ref{sec:remediation}). Methods using trained extractors, external provers, or multi-model ensembles fall outside scope.",
  "claims": [
    {
      "claim_id": "C3_process_kappa_definition",
      "text": "Process-level CoT cosine similarity quantifies reasoning homogeneity beyond answer-level kappa",
      "type": "methodological",
      "strength": "definition",
      "evidence": "Eq. process-sim",
      "exp_ids": [
        "exp-wild4-process-kappa"
      ]
    }
  ]
}

       ```latex
       \subsection{Process-Level Independence}
       \label{sec:process-kappa-method}

       Answer-level Fleiss' $\kappa$ may underestimate the true independence bottleneck. Two traces that follow nearly identical reasoning can reach different final answers; $\kappa$ treats such pairs as disagreeing, overestimating independence. We quantify process-level similarity via pairwise cosine similarity over TF-IDF representations of chain-of-thought traces. For problem $q$ with $K$ traces:
       \begin{equation}
       \label{eq:process-sim}
       \bar{s}(q) = \frac{2}{K(K{-}1)} \sum_{i < j} \cos(\mathbf{v}_i, \mathbf{v}_j),
       \end{equation}
       where $\mathbf{v}_i$ is the TF-IDF vector of trace $t_i$. When $\bar{s} \gg \kappa$, reasoning processes are more homogeneous than answers suggest. This metric applies to any trace aggregation method, not only SICA.
       ```

        "content": "\\section{Related Work}\n\\label{sec:related-work}\n\n\\paragraph{Self-Consistency and extensions.}\nSC selects the most frequent answer across chain-of-thought traces \\citep{wang2023sc}. Extensions include universal SC for free-form generation \\citep{chen2024usc}, confidence weighting \\citep{taubenfeld2025cisc}, diverse prompting with step-aware verification \\citep{li2023diverse}, and reasoning-aware filtering \\citep{wan2024rasc, kim2026reasc}. \\citet{loo2025reevaluating} show that SC gains plateau early. Our Condorcet analysis explains this: $\\kappa = 0.21$--$0.82$ compresses $K_{\\text{eff}}$ to 1.20--3.62 for $K = 12$ (\\S\\ref{sec:effective-k}).\n\n\\paragraph{Neuro-symbolic reasoning.}\nLogic-LM \\citep{pan2023logiclm} and LINC \\citep{olausson2023linc} translate problems into FOL for external provers; LINC documents ${\\sim}$40\\% translation errors. More recent systems extend this: DyLoS \\citep{xu2026dylos} selects solver types, Aristotle \\citep{li2025aristotle} applies logic-complete decomposition, FoVer \\citep{wang2025fover} trains FOL verifiers, and LogicAgent \\citep{luo2025logicagent} employs multi-perspective grounding. Each introduces capabilities beyond the reasoning model. \\citet{amonkar2025realitycheck} show that LLM formalization degrades with complexity, consistent with our findings. Our oracle controls confirm the aggregation mechanism is sound ($+14.22$\\,pp FOLIO, $+11.50$\\,pp ProofWriter); the failure localizes to self-extraction quality (\\S\\ref{sec:oracle}).\n\n\\paragraph{Test-time compute and verification.}\nScaling test-time computation through additional traces \\citep{snell2025scaling} faces the same correlation challenge: same-model attempts collapse into correlated solutions. Reasoning models intensify this (QwQ-32B: $C_0 = 2.6\\%$). Trained verifiers offer an alternative: outcome reward models \\citep{cobbe2021gsm8k}, process reward models \\citep{lightman2024prm}, and Math-Shepherd \\citep{wang2024mathshepherd}. Multi-agent debate refines answers through discussion \\citep{liang2023encouraging, du2023improving}. Self-correction without external feedback degrades performance \\citep{huang2024selfcorrect, stechly2025selfverification}, paralleling our finding.\n\n\\paragraph{CJT and ensemble diversity.}\nCJT \\citep{condorcet1785} establishes that independent majority voting converges to truth. \\citet{ladha1992correlated} prove correlated voters reduce effective ensemble size. \\citet{lefort2024condorcet_llm} apply CJT to LLM ensemble classification. We extend this to reasoning-trace aggregation, measuring $\\kappa$ and $K_{\\text{eff}}$ across 15 conditions (\\S\\ref{sec:effective-k}).",

GSM8K~\\citep{cobbe2021gsm8k} ($n{=}30$) and AIME 2024 ($n{=}22$) probe the degenerate regime ($C_0 < 5\\%$).\n\n\\paragraph{Parameters.} $K{=}12$ traces at $T{=}0.7$; extraction at $T_{\\text{ext}}{=}0.3$. Z3 timeout: 10s. $\\alpha{=}0.5$. Statistical significance via McNemar exact test ($\\alpha{=}0.05$); 95\\% bootstrap CIs from 10,000 resamples for $n \\geq 200$.\n\n\n\\subsection{Main Results}\n\\label{sec:main-results}\n\nTable~\\ref{tab:main_results} reports accuracy across all conditions sorted by SC baseline. SICA effectiveness depends sharply on the accuracy regime.\n\n\\begin{table*}[t]\n\\centering\n\\small\n\\caption{SICA vs.\\ SC across all conditions sorted by SC accuracy. $\\Delta$ = SICA $-$ SC (pp). $^b$In-context learning with gold FOL examples. $\\dagger$\\,$n < 100$ (suggestive only). \\textbf{Bold $\\Delta$}: $p < 0.05$ (McNemar). {*}\\,$p < 0.05$;\\enspace{**}\\,$p < 0.01$;\\enspace{***}\\,$p < 0.001$. Failure modes detailed in Appendix~\\ref{app:failure-modes}.}\n\\label{tab:main_results}\n\\begin{tabular}{@{}llrrrrl@{}}\n\\toprule\nModel & Domain & $n$ & SC (\\%) & SICA (\\%) & $\\Delta$ (pp) & $p$ \\\\\n\\midrule\n\\multicolumn{7}{l}{\\textit{Type C: error amplification (SC $\\leq$ 40\\%)}} \\\\\nMistral-7B & PW-D5 & 600 & 39.33 & 36.50 & $\\mathbf{-2.83}$ & 0.014{*} \\\\\n\\midrule\n\\multicolumn{7}{l}{\\textit{Intermediate SC (SC $\\sim$50--55\\%, Mistral-7B only)}} \\\\\nMistral-7B & LogiQA & 200 & 51.00 & 51.50 & $+$0.50 & 1.000 \\\\\nMistral-7B & FOLIO (s123) & 204 & 54.41 & 57.35 & $+$2.94 & 0.070 \\\\\nMistral-7B & FOLIO (s42) & 204 & 55.88 & \\textbf{59.31} & $+$3.43 & 0.092 \\\\\nMistral-7B & FOLIO (s456) & 204 & 55.88 & 55.88 & 0.00 & 1.000 \\\\\nMistral-7B & FOLIO (s789) & 204 & 54.41 & 53.92 & $-$0.49 & 1.000 \\\\\n\\midrule\n\\multicolumn{7}{l}{\\textit{Type A: confirmation bias (SC $\\geq$ 60\\%)}} \\\\\nLLaMA-3.1-8B & FOLIO & 204 & 66.18 & 61.76 & $-$4.41 & 0.093 \\\\\nLLaMA-3.1-8B & StrategyQA & 200 & 72.00 & 74.00 & $+$2.00 & 0.424 \\\\\nQwen2.5-14B & FOLIO (ICL)$^b$ & 199 & 74.37 & 74.87 & $+$0.50 & 1.000 \\\\\nQwen2.5-14B & PW-D5 & 600 & 75.17 & 72.67 & $\\mathbf{-2.50}$ & 0.011{*} \\\\\nQwen2.5-14B & FOLIO & 204 & 75.49 & 74.51 & $-$0.98 & 1.000 \\\\\nQwen2.5-7B & FOLIO & 204 & 75.98 & 76.47 & $+$0.49 & 1.000 \\\\\nQwen2.5-14B & LogiQA & 200 & 78.50 & 78.00 & $-$0.50 & 1.000 \\\\\nQwen3-14B & FOLIO & 204 & 84.80 & \\textbf{84.31} & $-$0.49 & 1.000 \\\\\nQwen3-14B & PW-D5 & 600 & 85.00 & 85.67 & $+$0.67 & 1.000 \\\\\n\\midrule\n\\multicolumn{7}{l}{\\textit{Degenerate ($C_0 < 5\\%$)}} \\\\\nQwQ-32B & AIME 2024$^\\dagger$ & 22 & 72.70 & 72.70 & 0.00 & \\textrm{---} \\\\\nQwen2.5-14B & GSM8K$^\\dagger$ & 30 & 96.67 & 96.67 & 0.00 & \\textrm{---} \\\\\n\\midrule\n\\multicolumn{7}{l}{\\textit{Oracle upper bound (gold FOL/DSL + Z3)}} \\\\\nQwen2.5-14B & FOLIO & 204 & 75.49 & \\textbf{89.71} & $\\mathbf{+14.22}$ & $2.0{\\times}10^{-7}${***} \\\\\nQwen2.5-14B & ProofWriter & 600 & 75.17 & \\textbf{86.67} & $\\mathbf{+11.50}$ & $9.21{\\times}10^{-15}${***} \\\\\n\\bottomrule\n\\end{tabular}\n\\end{table*}\n\n\\paragraph{Intermediate-SC region.} Mistral-7B on FOLIO (SC $\\sim$50--55\\%) shows positive directional trends in 2 of 4 seeds: $\\Delta = +3.43$\\,pp ($p = 0.092$) and $+2.94$\\,pp ($p = 0.070$); the remaining seeds show null or negative results. No seed reaches $p < 0.05$; the 4-seed mean is $+1.47$\\,pp. Mistral-7B on LogiQA, despite the same SC range, yields only $\\Delta = +0.50$\\,pp ($p = 1.0$), attributable to domain formalizability (Appendix~\\ref{app:failure-modes}).\n\n\\paragraph{Type A (SC $\\geq$ 60\\%): confirmation bias.} Nine conditions with SC $\\geq 63\\%$ (combined $N > 2{,}800$) produce no significant positive effects. Qwen2.5-14B on ProofWriter shows significant degradation ($\\Delta = -2.50$\\,pp, $p = 0.011$). Even in-context learning with gold FOL examples ($\\Delta = +0.50$\\,pp, n.s.) cannot break confirmation bias.\n\n\\paragraph{Type C (SC $\\leq$ 40\\%): error amplification.} Mistral-7B on ProofWriter ($n = 600$, SC $= 39.33\\%$) shows $\\Delta = -2.83$\\,pp ($p = 0.014$): SICA \\emph{significantly hurts} by formalizing incorrect reasoning.\n\n\\paragraph{Degenerate cases.} When $C_0 < 5\\%$, nearly all traces agree and SICA reduces to SC by construction.\n\nFigure~\\ref{fig:regime-scatter} visualizes the regime dependence: the intermediate-SC region is narrow and observed only for Mistral-7B on FOLIO.\n\n\\begin{figure}[t]\n\\centering\n\\includegraphics[width=\\columnwidth]{f2_regime_scatter}\n\\caption{SICA $\\Delta$ (pp) vs.\\ SC baseline. Filled: $n \\geq 100$; open: $n < 100$. The intermediate-SC region (50--55\\%, shaded) contains the only positive trends (none reaching $p < 0.05$). Type~C: significant degradation ($p = 0.014$).}\n\\label{fig:regime-scatter}\n\\end{figure}\n\n\n\\subsection{Oracle Control}\n\\label{sec:oracle}\n\nOracle variants replace self-extracted constraints with gold annotations: FOL from FOLIO ($n = 204$) and DSL from ProofWriter ($n = 600$). Oracle-SICA achieves 89.71\\% on FOLIO ($\\Delta = +14.22$\\,pp, $p = 2.0 \\times 10^{-7}$) and 86.67\\% on ProofWriter ($\\Delta = +11.50$\\,pp, $p = 9.21 \\times 10^{-15}$). Z3 agrees with gold labels on 95.1\\% (FOLIO) and 97.1\\% (ProofWriter) of problems.\n\nThese results establish that the MAX-SAT mechanism is sound given discriminative constraints. Mistral-7B's best self-extracted result ($+3.43$\\,pp) captures only 24.1\\% of the FOLIO oracle ceiling. The remaining 75.9\\% is lost to self-extraction quality.\n\n\n\\subsection{Four-Seed Replication}\n\\label{sec:replication}\n\nWe replicate the intermediate-SC result with four seeds on Mistral-7B FOLIO ($n = 204$, $K = 12$, $T = 0.7$). Effects range from $+3.43$ to $-0.49$\\,pp (mean $+1.47$\\,pp); 2 of 4 seeds show marginal trends ($p < 0.10$), none reaches $p < 0.05$. Seeds~42 and~456 share identical SC baselines (55.88\\%) yet produce different outcomes ($+3.43$ vs.\\ $0.00$\\,pp), indicating that seed-level constraint quality---not SC level alone---drives the effect. The entropy decomposition in Appendix~\\ref{app:entropy} confirms that the aggregate $\\Delta$ is driven by high-entropy problems: seed~42 achieves $+12.8$\\,pp on this partition while seed~456 shows $-1.3$\\,pp.",

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01VyXXpXUf3iLbt78fWXESUA`</summary>

```text
1	\section{Analysis}
2	\label{sec:analysis}
3	
4	The regime-dependent pattern in \S\ref{sec:main-results} establishes \emph{where} SICA succeeds and fails. This section investigates \emph{why}: trace correlation quantifies the SC plateau (\S\ref{sec:effective-k}), supporting diagnostics confirm the mechanism (Appendices~\ref{app:extraction}--\ref{app:entropy}), and remediation analysis reveals a fundamental independence-accuracy trade-off (\S\ref{sec:remediation}).
5	
6	\subsection{Condorcet Effective-K}
7	\label{sec:effective-k}
8	
9	Table~\ref{tab:independence} reports Fleiss' $\kappa$ and Effective-K (Eq.~\ref{eq:effk}) across 15 conditions at $K = 12$. Among instruction-tuned models, $\kappa$ ranges from 0.21 to 0.82, yielding $K_{\text{eff}} = 1.20\text{--}3.62$. Qwen3-14B in thinking mode reaches $\kappa = 0.91$ ($K_{\text{eff}} = 1.09$). The four Mistral-7B FOLIO seeds produce $\kappa \in [0.41, 0.51]$ ($K_{\text{eff}} \approx 1.8\text{--}2.2$), yet $\Delta$ ranges from $+3.43$ to $-0.49$\,pp---seed-level constraint quality, not independence, drives the variation. Two conditions are jointly necessary for positive $\Delta$: sufficient $K_{\text{eff}}$ (Qwen conditions with $K_{\text{eff}} \leq 1.30$ never improve) and sufficient SC accuracy (Mistral on ProofWriter with the highest $K_{\text{eff}} = 3.62$ but SC $= 39\%$ produces significant degradation). Even when both hold, sampling variance determines whether the constraint pool is discriminative. Cross-model validation with three families confirms universality: Mistral-7B ($\kappa = 0.44$), LLaMA-3.1-8B ($\kappa = 0.41$), Qwen2.5-7B ($\kappa = 0.66$), and Qwen2.5-14B ($\kappa = 0.74$) all produce $\kappa$ well above zero (Table~\ref{tab:kappa_generalization}; Appendix~\ref{app:cross-model-kappa}).
10	
11	\begin{table}[t]
